mwp_d21_budget_refresh_effect = {
remove_variable = mwp_d21b_ready
save_scope_as = mwp_d21b_player
clear_variable_list = mwp_d21b_seen_regiments
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_1 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_levies_1 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_1 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_2 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_levies_2 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_2 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_3 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_levies_3 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_3 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_4 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_levies_4 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_4 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_5 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_levies_5 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_5 value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_unknown value = 0 }
every_maa_regiment = {
limit = {
 NOT = { scope:mwp_d21b_player = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21b_seen_regiments target = prev } } }
 OR = { regiment_owner = scope:mwp_d21b_player AND = { is_title_maa_regiment = yes regiment_owning_title = { holder = scope:mwp_d21b_player } } }
 }
 save_scope_as = mwp_d21b_seen_regiment
 scope:mwp_d21b_player = { add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_d21b_seen_regiments target = scope:mwp_d21b_seen_regiment } }
 save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21b_size value = maa_size }
 save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21b_is_title value = 0 }
 if = { limit = { is_title_maa_regiment = yes } save_scope_value_as = { name = mwp_d21b_is_title value = 1 } }
if = {
limit = {
maa_size > 0
}
if = {
limit = {
is_maa_type = huscarl
}
scope:mwp_d21b_player = {
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_type_currency value = mwp_d21_upkeep_currency_1 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_type_bound value = mwp_d21_upkeep_bound_1 }
if = {
limit = {
scope:mwp_d21b_is_title = 1 government_allows = replace_gold_cost_by_treasury
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_type_currency value = 4 }

}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_d21b_type_currency = 1
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_1 add = { value = var:mwp_d21b_type_bound multiply = scope:mwp_d21b_size } }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_d21b_type_currency = 2
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_full_maa_2 add = { value = var:mwp_d21b_type_bound multiply = scope:mwp_d21b_size } }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_d21b_type_currency = 3
}

}

}

}

limit = {
mwp_auto_budget_mode_value = 1
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_4 value = { value = var:mwp_d21b_balance_4 add = var:mwp_d21b_current_maa_4 subtract = var:mwp_d21b_full_maa_4 subtract = var:mwp_d21b_levies_4 subtract = 0.01 } }

}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_4 value = { value = var:mwp_d21b_headroom_4 max = { value = var:mwp_d21b_balance_4 subtract = 0.01 } } }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_5 value = { value = var:mwp_d21b_income_5 multiply = mwp_auto_income_fraction_value subtract = var:mwp_d21b_full_maa_5 subtract = 0.01 } }
if = {
limit = {
mwp_auto_budget_mode_value = 1
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_5 value = { value = var:mwp_d21b_balance_5 add = var:mwp_d21b_current_maa_5 subtract = var:mwp_d21b_full_maa_5 subtract = var:mwp_d21b_levies_5 subtract = 0.01 } }

}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_5 value = { value = var:mwp_d21b_headroom_5 max = { value = var:mwp_d21b_balance_5 subtract = 0.01 } } }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_owner value = this }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_generation value = var:mwp_generation }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_day value = days_since_birth }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_mode value = mwp_auto_budget_mode_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_epoch value = var:mwp_d21r_epoch }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_stamp value = var:mwp_d21r_clock }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_counter value = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_initial_prestige_maa value = monthly_character_men_at_arms_expense_prestige }
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_ready value = yes }

}

}
mwp_d21_budget_accept_purchase_effect = {
if = {
limit = {
has_variable = mwp_d21b_ready var:mwp_d21b_ready = yes has_variable = mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency has_variable = mwp_script_selected_upkeep
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency = 1
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_1 subtract = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_1 add = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency = 2
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_2 subtract = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_2 add = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency = 3
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_3 subtract = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_3 add = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency = 4
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_4 subtract = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_4 add = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep_currency = 5
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_headroom_5 subtract = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
change_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_reserved_5 add = var:mwp_script_selected_upkeep }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_d21b_counter value = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value }

}
